gen(poly,reflected):\n    t=[]\n    for i in range(256):\n        c=i\n        if reflected:\n            for _ in range(8): c=(c>>1)^poly if c&1 else c>>1\n        else:\n            for _ in range(8): c=((c<<1)^poly)&0xFF if c&0x80 else (c<<1)&0xFF\n        t.append(c)\n    return t\ndef refl(x):\n    r=0\n    for i in range(8):\n        if (1<<i)&x: r|=1<<(7-i)\n    return r\nprint('tbl matches nonreflected poly 0x31:', gen(0x31,False)==tbl)\nprint('tbl matches reflected  poly 0x8C:', gen(0x8C,True)==tbl)\nprint('tbl matches nonreflected poly 0x07:', gen(0x07,False)==tbl)\nprint('tbl matches nonreflected poly 0x1D:', gen(0x1D,False)==tbl)\ndef app(data):\n    crc=0\n    for b in data:\n        crc=tbl[refl(b)^crc]\n    return refl(crc)\ndef model(data,poly,init,refin,refout):\n    crc=init\n    for b in data:\n        if refin: b=refl(b)\n        crc^=b\n        for _ in range(8):\n            crc=((crc<<1)^poly)&0xFF if crc&0x80 else (crc<<1)&0xFF\n    if refout: crc=refl(crc)\n    return crc\ntests=[b'\\x01',b'\\x02',b'123456789',bytes([0xA3,0xA4,0x01,0x50,0x01,0x01])]\nfor t in tests: print(t.hex(),hex(app(t)))\n# find equivalent model\nfor poly in [0x31,0x07,0x1D,0x8C,0x9B,0x2F]:\n    for refin in [0,1]:\n        for refout in [0,1]:\n            if all(app(t)==model(t,poly,0,refin,refout) for t in tests): print('equiv poly=%02X refin=%d refout=%d init=0'%(poly,refin,refout))\nprint('CRC8/MAXIM 123456789 ->', hex(model(b'123456789',0x31,0,1,1)))\nprint('CRC8/ITU  123456789 ->', hex(model(b'123456789',0x07,0,0,0)))\n\""
